Program

col_2019 = [c for c in df.columns if pd.notna(df.iloc[3, c]) and '2019' in str(df.iloc[3, c])][0]

regions = ['Northeast', 'Midwest', 'South', 'West']
rates = {}
for r in range(len(df)):
    label = str(df.iloc[r, 0]).strip()
    if label in regions:
        rates[label] = float(df.iloc[r, col_2019])

result = max(rates, key=rates.get)

FF-0008healthdatedate_period

In which survey year reported between 1984 and 2019 was the total uninsured percentage for people under 65 at its highest?

Gold: 2010

Verdict: Right
Comments: Compared year estimates, excluding SE columns. 2010: 18.2; 1994: 17.5; 1997: 17.5; 2009: 17.5. Source: A!B4:BD4,B8:BD8.

Workbook: workbooks/nchs_hus_uninsured.xlsx · sheet A
Source: download · page · CDC NCHS (Health, United States 2020-2021)

Program
import re
import pandas as pd

df = pd.read_excel('nchs_hus_uninsured.xlsx', sheet_name='A', header=None)
header = df.iloc[3]
# Row index 7 corresponds to Total under 65 years (percent of population)
row_idx = 7

year_rates = {}
for c in range(1, len(df.columns)):
    h = str(header[c])
    if h != 'SE' and pd.notna(header[c]):
        m = re.search(r'\b(19\d\d|20\d\d)\b', h)
        if m:
            yr = m.group(1)
            val = float(df.iloc[row_idx, c])
            if yr not in year_rates or val > year_rates[yr]:
                year_rates[yr] = val

result = max(year_rates, key=year_rates.get)
Independent check

Check value: 2010 · generator stated: 2010

import pandas as pd
import re

df = pd.read_excel('nchs_hus_uninsured.xlsx', sheet_name='A', header=None)
# Row 8 (index 7) has Percent of population for Total, under 65 years
# Row 4 (index 3) has year labels, SE columns alternate
headers = df.iloc[3]
row_vals = df.iloc[7]
year_rates = {}
for col_idx in range(1, len(headers)):
    h = str(headers[col_idx])
    if h != 'SE' and h != 'nan':
        match = re.search(r'\b(19\d\d|20\d\d)\b', h)
        if match:
            year = match.group(1)
            val = pd.to_numeric(row_vals[col_idx], errors='coerce')
            if pd.notna(val):
                if year not in year_rates or val > year_rates[year]:
                    year_rates[year] = val
result = max(year_rates, key=year_rates.get)
Your follow-up review

import pandas as pd

df = pd.read_excel('cms_nhe_retail_prescription_drugs.xlsx', sheet_name='Table 16', header=None)

# Percent change section is rows 33 to 61 (0-indexed 33 to 60)
pct_change_rows = df.iloc[33:61].copy()

def parse_year(val):
    s = str(val).strip()
    try:
        return int(float(s[:4]))
    except:
        return None

pct_change_rows['year'] = pct_change_rows[0].apply(parse_year)
subset = pct_change_rows[(pct_change_rows['year'] >= 2015) & (pct_change_rows['year'] <= 2024)]
# Out of pocket is column C (index 2)
oop_growth = pd.to_numeric(subset[2], errors='coerce')
result = int((oop_growth < 0).sum())

import pandas as pd

df = pd.read_excel('cms_program_stats_premiums_2023.xlsx', sheet_name='MDCR PREMIUMS 4_CPS_04PRM', header=None)

# Identify Total Part B Premium Amount column
total_col = None

    if 'Total Part B Premium Amount' in str(df.iloc[2, c]):
        total_col = c
        break

# Data rows are 2018 to 2023
data = []
for r in range(4, 11):
    val_year = str(df.iloc[r, 0]).strip()
    if val_year.isdigit():

years_df[0] = years_df[0].astype(str).str.replace(r'\.0$', '', regex=True).str.strip()
years_df[10] = pd.to_numeric(years_df[10])
min_row = years_df.loc[years_df[10].idxmin()]
result = str(min_row[0])

import pandas as pd

df_2024 = df[(df['Year'] == 2024) & (~df['State'].str.contains('TOTAL', case=False, na=False))].copy()

    if pd.isna(x) or str(x).strip() in ['--', '', '-']:
        return 0
    return int(str(x).replace(',', '').strip())

    (df['Year'] == 2021) &
    (df['Sex'].isin(['M', 'Male'])) &
    (df['Race Ethnicity'] == 'Hispanic') &
    (df['Leading Cause'].str.contains('Covid', case=False))
]['Age Adjusted Death Rate'].values[0])

    (df['Year'] == 2021) &
    (df['Sex'].isin(['F', 'Female'])) &
    (df['Race Ethnicity'] == 'Non-Hispanic Black') &
    (df['Leading Cause'].str.contains('Covid', case=False))
]['Age Adjusted Death Rate'].values[0])

Check value: ["California", "Texas", "Pennsylvania"] · generator stated: ["California", "Texas", "Pennsylvania"]

import pandas as pd

df = pd.read_excel('census_state_government_finances_2022.xlsx', sheet_name='2022 ASFIN', header=None)
header_row = df.iloc[4].astype(str).str.replace('\xa0', ' ').str.strip()
row_labels = df.iloc[:, 0].astype(str).str.replace('\xa0', ' ').str.strip()

hosp_row = row_labels[row_labels == 'Hospitals'].index[0]

state_cols = [c for c in range(2, df.shape[1]) if header_row[c] not in ['United States', 'nan']]
spending = {}
for c in state_cols:
    val = df.iat[hosp_row, c]
    try:
        spending[df.iat[4, c]] = float(val)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        continue

top_3 = sorted(spending.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[:3]
result = [item[0] for item in top_3]

row_labels = df.iloc[:, 0].astype(str).str.replace('\xa0', ' ').str.strip()
highway_row = row_labels[row_labels == 'Highways'].index[0]

fl_val = float(df.iat[highway_row, fl_col])
ga_val = float(df.iat[highway_row, ga_col])

result = df.iat[4, fl_col] if fl_val > ga_val else df.iat[4, ga_col]
Your follow-up review

FF-0211othernamecomparison

In calendar year 2012, between withholding taxes and sales and use taxes, which source generated more total revenue?

Gold: Withholding

Verdict: Right
Comments: Right on the published accounting basis: withholding $5,131,695,119.65 exceeds sales and use $3,805,046,420.28. July 2012 has blank cells for both columns because $477,113,000 of withholding and $438,055,000 of sales/use receipts were accrued to the fiscal year ending June 30, 2012 and are already included in June's final figures. Adding those July amounts again would double-count them. August-December sums also match December's fiscal YTD totals exactly. Source: CSV, Calendar Year=2012, Withholding/Sales and Use. July accrual schedule (p. 2): source document ; June final statement (p. 1): source document ; December YTD statement (p. 1): source document

Workbook: workbooks/ct_tax_revenue_by_month.csv
Source: download · page · Connecticut Department of Revenue Services (data.ct.gov)

Program
import pandas as pd

df = pd.read_csv('ct_tax_revenue_by_month.csv')
df_2012 = df[df['Calendar Year'] == 2012]
withholding_total = df_2012['Withholding'].sum()
sales_total = df_2012['Sales and Use'].sum()
result = 'Withholding' if withholding_total > sales_total else 'Sales and Use'
Independent check

Check value: Withholding · generator stated: Withholding
